That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import posts, settings, users\nfrom app.routes import auth, posts as posts_routes, user_password, users as users_routes\nfrom app.security import SESSION_SECONDS, AdminRequired, LoginRequired, PasswordChangeRequired, csrf_token, current_user\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"Chi Chapter CMS\")\n    app.state.database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    users.init_db(app.state.database)\n    posts.init_posts(app.state.database)\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       max_age=SESSION_SECONDS, same_site=\"lax\")\n\n    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    def send_to_login(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(PasswordChangeRequired)\n    def must_change(request: Request, exc: PasswordChangeRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/user/password\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(AdminRequired)\n    def admin_only(request: Request, exc: AdminRequired):\n        db = request.app.state.database\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/forbidden.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"Admin only\", \"admin\": users.current_admin(db),\n             \"contact_line\": users.get_setting(db, \"contact_line\")},\n            status_code=403)\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(user_password.router)\n    app.include_router(users_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(posts_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request)}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"subtitle\": settings.SITE_SUBTITLE,\n             \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"One place that decides \"may this User do this to this item\".\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\n\ndef is_admin(user) -> bool:\n    return user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef can_edit_post(user, post) -> bool:\n    return is_admin(user) or post[\"author_id\"] == user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef can_delete_post(user, post) -> bool:\n    if is_admin(user):\n        return True\n    return post[\"author_id\"] == user[\"id\"] and post[\"status\"] == \"draft\"\n\n\ndef can_edit_slug(user, post) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Admin always; an Editor only while the Slug is not locked.\"\"\"\n    return is_admin(user) or not post[\"slug_locked\"]\n\n\ndef can_unlock_slug(user) -> bool:\n    return is_admin(user)\n\"\"\"Post storage. A Post's Byline is copied from its author at first Published.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.users import connect\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists posts (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    slug text not null unique,\n    body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references users(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    published_at text,\n    slug_locked integer not null default 0,\n    byline_name text,\n    byline_title text,\n    byline_term text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", title).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", text.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef init_posts(database: Path) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef get_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id where posts.id = ?\",\n            (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id\"\n            \" order by posts.updated_at desc, posts.id desc\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef create_post(database: Path, *, author, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at,\"\n                \" updated_at) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, author[\"id\"], stamp, stamp))\n            post_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            return post_id\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef update_post(database: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            if slug is None:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, updated_at=?\"\n                             \" where id=?\", (title, body, stamp, post_id))\n            else:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, slug=?,\"\n                             \" updated_at=? where id=?\",\n                             (title, body, slug or slugify(title), stamp, post_id))\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                author = conn.execute(\n                    \"select users.* from users join posts\"\n                    \" on posts.author_id = users.id where posts.id=?\",\n                    (post_id,)).fetchone()\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            elif action == \"draft\":\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set status='draft' where id=?\",\n                             (post_id,))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, author, stamp: str) -> None:\n    first = conn.execute(\"select published_at from posts where id=?\",\n                         (post_id,)).fetchone()[\"published_at\"] is None\n    conn.execute(\"update posts set status='published' where id=?\", (post_id,))\n    if first:  # published timestamp, Byline and Slug lock are fixed at first publish\n        conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set published_at=?, slug_locked=1, byline_name=?,\"\n            \" byline_title=?, byline_term=? where id=?\",\n            (stamp, author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"], author[\"term\"],\n             post_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update posts set slug_locked=0 where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef format_byline(name, title, term) -> str:\n    return \", \".join(p for p in (name, title, term) if p)\n\n\ndef byline(post) -> str:\n    return format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                         post[\"byline_term\"])\n\"\"\"User accounts: storage, password hashing, and credential checks.\n\nPasswords are only ever stored as argon2 hashes. Nothing here logs a password.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerifyMismatchError\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists users (\n    id integer primary key,\n    email text not null unique,\n    display_name text not null,\n    title text not null default '',\n    term text not null default '',\n    role text not null check (role in ('admin', 'editor')),\n    is_active integer not null default 1,\n    password_hash text not null,\n    must_change_password integer not null default 0\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n_SETTINGS_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists settings (\n    key text primary key,\n    value text not null\n)\n\"\"\"\n\nMIN_PASSWORD = 12\n\n\ndef password_problem(password: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"The one place the password rule lives: a message if it fails, else None.\"\"\"\n    if len(password) < MIN_PASSWORD:\n        return f\"The password must be at least {MIN_PASSWORD} characters.\"\n    return None\n\n\nclass LastAdmin(Exception):\n    \"\"\"The change would leave the chapter with no active Admin.\"\"\"\n\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so both failures take similar time.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef connect(database: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(database)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(database: Path) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n        columns = {r[\"name\"] for r in conn.execute(\"pragma table_info(users)\")}\n        if \"must_change_password\" not in columns:\n            conn.execute(\"alter table users add column must_change_password\"\n                         \" integer not null default 0\")\n        conn.execute(_SETTINGS_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef create_user(database: Path, *, email: str, password: str, role: str,\n                display_name: str, title: str = \"\", term: str = \"\") -> int:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"role must be one of {ROLES}\")\n    problem = password_problem(password)\n    if problem:\n        raise ValueError(problem)\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"insert into users (email, display_name, title, term, role,\"\n            \" password_hash) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (email.strip().lower(), display_name, title, term, role,\n             _hasher.hash(password)),\n        )\n        return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_user(database: Path, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select * from users where id = ?\",\n                            (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef authenticate(database: Path, email: str, password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the active user for these credentials, or None for any failure.\"\"\"\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        user = conn.execute(\"select * from users where email = ?\",\n                            (email.strip().lower(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerifyMismatchError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user_by_email(database: Path, email: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select * from users where email = ?\",\n                            (email.strip().lower(),)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef set_password(database: Path, user_id: int, password: str, *,\n                 temporary: bool = False) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Store a new argon2 hash. A temporary one must be changed at next login.\"\"\"\n    problem = password_problem(password)\n    if problem:\n        raise ValueError(problem)\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update users set password_hash = ?,\"\n                     \" must_change_password = ? where id = ?\",\n                     (_hasher.hash(password), int(temporary), user_id))\n\n\ndef verify_password(user: sqlite3.Row, password: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(user[\"password_hash\"], password)\n    except (VerifyMismatchError, InvalidHashError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef make_temporary_password() -> str:\n    return secrets.token_urlsafe(12)\n\n\ndef list_users(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select * from users order by is_active desc, display_name\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef _other_active_admins(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> int:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"select count(*) from users where role = 'admin' and is_active = 1\"\n        \" and id != ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()[0]\n\n\ndef _would_orphan_admin(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True if user_id is the only active Admin.\"\"\"\n    current = conn.execute(\"select role, is_active from users where id = ?\",\n                           (user_id,)).fetchone()\n    return bool(current and current[\"role\"] == \"admin\" and current[\"is_active\"]\n                and _other_active_admins(conn, user_id) == 0)\n\n\ndef update_user(database: Path, user_id: int, *, display_name: str, title: str,\n                term: str, role: str) -> None:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"role must be one of {ROLES}\")\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        if role != \"admin\" and _would_orphan_admin(conn, user_id):\n            raise LastAdmin\n        conn.execute(\"update users set display_name = ?, title = ?, term = ?,\"\n                     \" role = ? where id = ?\",\n                     (display_name, title, term, role, user_id))\n\n\ndef deactivate_user(database: Path, user_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deactivate, never delete: Posts and Bylines stay.\"\"\"\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        if _would_orphan_admin(conn, user_id):\n            raise LastAdmin\n        conn.execute(\"update users set is_active = 0 where id = ?\", (user_id,))\n\n\ndef current_admin(database: Path) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select * from users where role = 'admin' and is_active = 1\"\n            \" order by id desc limit 1\").fetchone()\n\n\ndef get_setting(database: Path, key: str, default: str = \"\") -> str:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        row = conn.execute(\"select value from settings where key = ?\",\n                           (key,)).fetchone()\n    return row[\"value\"] if row else default\n\n\ndef set_setting(database: Path, key: str, value: str) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"insert into settings (key, value) {"parentUuid":"9854af36-e054-40f9-af47-7a7ad753bd47","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9100aa34-3880-4efe-933a-856c1a2fa5c8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ShA5DPwXLu3Q5yhsYD1W9y","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Posts: list, create, edit, publish, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import authz, posts, users\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, current_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\nguarded = [Depends(current_user), Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\nACTIONS = {\"save\", \"publish\", \"draft\"}\n\n\ndef load_post(request: Request, user, post_id: int, allowed) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Fetch a Post the current User is allowed to act on, or 404/403.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    if not allowed(user, post):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"You can only change your own Posts.\")\n    return post\n\n\ndef render_form(request: Request, user, *, post=None, form=None, error=None,\n                status_code=200):\n    if form is None:\n        form = ({\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": post[\"body\"]}\n                if post else {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post else \"New post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form\": form, \"error\": error, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"byline\": posts.byline(post) if post and post[\"published_at\"] else None,\n         \"can_edit_slug\": authz.can_edit_slug(user, post) if post else True,\n         \"can_unlock_slug\": bool(post and post[\"slug_locked\"]\n                                 and authz.can_unlock_slug(user)),\n         \"can_delete\": bool(post and authz.can_delete_post(user, post))},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/posts.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"posts\": posts.list_posts(request.app.state.database),\n         \"can_edit\": authz.can_edit_post})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_form(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return render_form(request, user)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef preview(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n            body: str = Form(\"\"), post_id: int | None = Form(None)):\n    author = user\n    byline = None\n    if post_id is not None:\n        post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n        author = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, post[\"author_id\"])\n        byline = posts.byline(post) if post[\"published_at\"] else None\n    if byline is None:\n        byline = posts.format_byline(author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"],\n                                     author[\"term\"])\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/preview.html\",\n        {\"title\": title or \"Untitled\", \"html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"byline\": byline})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n           slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, error=\"A Post needs a title.\",\n                           status_code=400)\n    try:\n        post_id = posts.create_post(\n            request.app.state.database, author=user, title=title.strip(),\n            slug=posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else \"\", body=body,\n            publish=action == \"publish\")\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id:int}\")\ndef edit_form(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n    return render_form(request, user, post=post)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef save(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n         title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n         action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n    if action not in ACTIONS:\n        raise HTTPException(400, \"Unknown action.\")\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    new_slug = None\n    if authz.can_edit_slug(user, post):\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else post[\"slug\"]\n    try:\n        posts.update_post(request.app.state.database, post_id, title=title.strip(),\n                          body=body, slug=new_slug, action=action)\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/unlock-slug\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef unlock_slug(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    if not authz.can_unlock_slug(user):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Only the Admin can unlock a Slug.\")\n    if posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id) is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    posts.unlock_slug(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/delete\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef delete(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_delete_post)\n    posts.delete_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Admin-only user management. Every route needs the Admin role.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, require_admin, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/users\")\nguarded = [Depends(require_admin), Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\nLAST_ADMIN = (\"This is the last active Admin. Promote a successor to Admin \"\n              \"first, then try again.\")\n\n\ndef render(request: Request, template: str, status_code: int = 200, **ctx):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, template,\n        {\"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request), \"roles\": users.ROLES,\n         \"min_password\": users.MIN_PASSWORD, **ctx}, status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load_user(request: Request, user_id: int):\n    found = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id)\n    if found is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such user.\")\n    return found\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef users_list(request: Request, user=Depends(require_admin), notice: str = \"\"):\n    db = request.app.state.database\n    return render(request, \"admin/users.html\", title=\"Users\", user=user,\n                  users=users.list_users(db), notice=notice,\n                  contact_line=users.get_setting(db, \"contact_line\"))\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef user_new(request: Request, user=Depends(require_admin)):\n    return render(request, \"admin/user_new.html\", title=\"New user\", user=user,\n                  form={\"role\": \"editor\"}, error=None)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(require_admin),\n           email: str = Form(\"\"), display_name: str = Form(\"\"),\n           title: str = Form(\"\"), term: str = Form(\"\"), role: str = Form(\"editor\"),\n           password: str = Form(\"\")):\n    form = {\"email\": email, \"display_name\": display_name, \"title\": title,\n            \"term\": term, \"role\": role}\n\n    def fail(message: str):\n        return render(request, \"admin/user_new.html\", 400, title=\"New user\",\n                      user=user, form=form, error=message)\n\n    if not email.strip() or not display_name.strip():\n        return fail(\"Email and display name are required.\")\n    if role not in users.ROLES:\n        return fail(\"Choose Admin or Editor.\")\n    problem = users.password_problem(password)\n    if problem:\n        return fail(problem)\n    try:\n        users.create_user(request.app.state.database, email=email,\n                          password=password, role=role,\n                          display_name=display_name.strip(), title=title.strip(),\n                          term=term.strip())\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        return fail(\"That email already has an account.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/users\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/contact\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef save_contact(request: Request, contact_line: str = Form(\"\")):\n    users.set_setting(request.app.state.database, \"contact_line\",\n                      contact_line.strip())\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/users\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{user_id:int}\")\ndef user_edit(request: Request, user_id: int, user=Depends(require_admin)):\n    target = load_user(request, user_id)\n    return render(request, \"admin/user_edit.html\", title=\"Edit user\", user=user,\n                  target=target, error=None)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{user_id:int}\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef save(request: Request, user_id: int, user=Depends(require_admin),\n         display_name: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"), term: str = Form(\"\"),\n         role: str = Form(\"editor\")):\n    target = load_user(request, user_id)\n    error = None\n    if not display_name.strip():\n        error = \"A display name is required.\"\n    elif role not in users.ROLES:\n        error = \"Choose Admin or Editor.\"\n    else:\n        try:\n            users.update_user(request.app.state.database, user_id,\n                              display_name=display_name.strip(),\n                              title=title.strip(), term=term.strip(), role=role)\n        except users.LastAdmin:\n            error = LAST_ADMIN\n    if error:\n        return render(request, \"admin/user_edit.html\", 400, title=\"Edit user\",\n                      user=user, target=target, error=error)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/users\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{user_id:int}/deactivate\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef deactivate(request: Request, user_id: int, user=Depends(require_admin)):\n    target = load_user(request, user_id)\n    try:\n        users.deactivate_user(request.app.state.database, user_id)\n    except users.LastAdmin:\n        return render(request, \"admin/user_edit.html\", 400, title=\"Edit user\",\n                      user=user, target=target, error=LAST_ADMIN)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/users\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{user_id:int}/reset-password\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef reset_password(request: Request, user_id: int, user=Depends(require_admin)):\n    \"\"\"Set a temporary password and show it once, in this response only.\"\"\"\n    target = load_user(request, user_id)\n    if target[\"id\"] == user[\"id\"]:\n        return render(request, \"admin/user_edit.html\", 400, title=\"Edit user\",\n                      user=user, target=target,\n                      error=\"To reset your own password, use your own \"\n                            \"password page or run `cms reset-password` on the machine.\")\n    temp = users.make_temporary_password()\n    users.set_password(request.app.state.database, user_id, temp, temporary=True)\n    response = render(request, \"admin/user_temp_password.html\", title=\"Temporary password\",\n                      user=user, target=target, temp_password=temp)\n    response.headers[\"Cache-Control\"] = \"no-store\"\n    return response\n\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF protection and the login requirement for admin routes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import users\n\nSESSION_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous (or Deactivated) visitor; becomes a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing route: reject a missing/wrong token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user_id: int) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user_id\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\nclass PasswordChangeRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A User on a temporary password must set their own first.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef logged_in_user(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, active user, or redirect to login.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired\n    return user\n\n\ndef current_user(user=Depends(logged_in_user)):\n    \"\"\"Like logged_in_user, but a temporary password blocks everything else.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"must_change_password\"]:\n        raise PasswordChangeRequired\n    return user\n\n\nclass AdminRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for a logged-in non-Admin on an admin-only URL; becomes a 403 page.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef require_admin(user=Depends(current_user)):\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every admin-only route. Anonymous users redirect first.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise AdminRequired\n    return user\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom markupsafe import escape\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import format_byline\nfrom app.users import connect\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\nclass DraftInOutput(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A Draft's title or Slug would appear in the generated site.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _posts_where(database: Path, where: str) -> list:\n    if not database.exists():\n        return []\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        if not conn.execute(\"select 1 from sqlite_master where name='posts'\").fetchone():\n            return []\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"select * from posts where {where} order by published_at desc, id desc\"\n        ).fetchall()\n\n\ndef _published_posts(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status='published'\")\n\n\ndef _draft_rows(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status!='published'\")\n\n\ndef _first_paragraph(html: str) -> str:\n    match = re.search(r\"<p>.*?</p>\", html, re.S)\n    return match.group(0) if match else \"\"\n\n\ndef _check_no_drafts(site: Path, database: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Refuse if any Draft made it into any page of the site.\n\n    Every post entry carries a data-post-id marker and links to\n    posts/<slug>.html. Both are compared with the Drafts straight from the\n    database (ids and Slugs are unique), on every file, so a Draft leaking onto\n    Home or News is caught as surely as one on its own page. Titles are not\n    matched: a Draft called \"News\" must not block every publish.\n    \"\"\"\n    drafts = _draft_rows(database)\n    for f in sorted(site.rglob(\"*\")):\n        if not f.is_file():\n            continue\n        text = f.read_text()\n        marked = {int(i) for i in re.findall(r'data-post-id=\"(\\d+)\"', text)}\n        for draft in drafts:\n            if (draft[\"id\"] in marked or f\"posts/{draft['slug']}.html\" in text\n                    or f.name == f\"{draft['slug']}.html\" and f.parent.name == \"posts\"):\n                raise DraftInOutput(\n                    f\"Refusing to publish: the Draft \\\"{draft['title']}\\\" would \"\n                    f\"appear in {f.relative_to(site)}. Drafts must never leave the \"\n                    \"database. The site was not written.\")\n\n\ndef render(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> tuple[Path, list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Render Published content into `out`; return it and a list of rendered items.\n\n    Builds in a sibling folder and swaps it in only after the Draft check\n    passes, so a refusal never leaves a half-finished site.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    work = out.parent / (out.name + \".building\")\n    if work.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(work)\n    work.mkdir(parents=True)\n    try:\n        items = _render_into(work, database)\n    except BaseException:\n        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)\n        raise\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    work.rename(out)\n    return out, items\n\n\ndef _render_into(work: Path, database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    env = environment()\n    published = _published_posts(database)\n    entries = []\n    for post in published:\n        body = render_markdown(post[\"body\"])\n        entries.append({\n            \"id\": post[\"id\"], \"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": body,\n            \"excerpt\": _first_paragraph(body),\n            \"date\": (post[\"published_at\"] or \"\")[:10],\n            \"byline\": format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                                    post[\"byline_term\"]),\n            \"href\": f\"posts/{post['slug']}.html\",\n        })\n\n    (work / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (work / \"posts\").mkdir()\n    nav = {\"home_path\": \"index.html\", \"news_path\": \"news.html\"}\n\n    def write(path: str, template: str, prefix: str, **ctx) -> None:\n        # prefix is \"\" for top-level pages and \"../\" for pages in posts/\n        (work / path).write_text(env.get_template(template).render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, subtitle=settings.SITE_SUBTITLE,\n            css_path=prefix + \"style.css\",\n            home_path=prefix + nav[\"home_path\"], news_path=prefix + nav[\"news_path\"],\n            **ctx))\n\n    write(\"index.html\", \"public/home.html\", \"\", items=entries[:5])\n    write(\"news.html\", \"public/news.html\", \"\", items=entries)\n    for e in entries:\n        write(e[\"href\"], \"public/post.html\", \"../\", post=e)\n    _check_no_drafts(work, database)\n    return [\"Home (index.html)\", \"News (news.html)\"] + [\n        f\"Post: {e['title']} ({e['href']})\" for e in entries]\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    return render(out, database)[0]\n\"\"\"Markdown -> sanitized HTML. Preview and the Publish run both call this.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": True})\n\n_TAGS = {\"p\", \"br\", \"h1\", \"h2\", \"h3\", \"h4\", \"h5\", \"h6\", \"strong\", \"em\", \"ul\", \"ol\",\n         \"li\", \"blockquote\", \"a\", \"code\", \"pre\", \"hr\"}\n_ATTRIBUTES = {\"a\": {\"href\", \"title\"}}\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render Markdown, then strip everything outside the allowlist.\n\n    Raw HTML is removed (scripts, event handlers, images). csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing route: reject a missing/wrong token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user_id: int) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user_id\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\nclass PasswordChangeRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A User on a temporary password must set their own first.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef user\n\n\ndef current_user(user=Depends(logged_in_user)):\n    \"\"\"Like logged_in_user, but a temporary password blocks everything else.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"must_change_password\"]:\n        raise A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom markupsafe import escape\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import format_byline\nfrom app.users import connect\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\nclass DraftInOutput(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A Draft's title or Slug would appear in the generated site.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _posts_where(database: Path, where: str) -> list:\n    if not database.exists():\n        return []\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        if not conn.execute(\"select 1 from sqlite_master where name='posts'\").fetchone():\n            return []\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"select * from posts where {where} order by published_at desc, id desc\"\n        ).fetchall()\n\n\ndef _published_posts(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status='published'\")\n\n\ndef _draft_rows(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status!='published'\")\n\n\ndef _first_paragraph(html: str) -> str:\n    match = re.search(r\"<p>.*?</p>\", html, re.S)\n    return match.group(0) if match else \"\"\n\n\ndef _check_no_drafts(site: Path, database: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Refuse if any Draft made it into any page of the site.\n\n    Every post entry carries a data-post-id marker and links to\n    posts/<slug>.html. Both are compared with the Drafts straight from the\n    database (ids and Slugs are unique), on every file, so a Draft leaking onto\n    Home or News is caught as surely as one on its own page. Titles are not\n    matched: a Draft called \"News\" must not block every publish.\n    \"\"\"\n    drafts = _draft_rows(database)\n    for f in sorted(site.rglob(\"*\")):\n        if not f.is_file():\n            continue\n        text = f.read_text()\n        marked = {int(i) for i in re.findall(r'data-post-id=\"(\\d+)\"', text)}\n        for draft in drafts:\n            if (draft[\"id\"] in marked or f\"posts/{draft['slug']}.html\" in text\n                    or f.name == f\"{draft['slug']}.html\" and f.parent.name == \"posts\"):\n                raise DraftInOutput(\n                    f\"Refusing to publish: the Draft \\\"{draft['title']}\\\" would \"\n                    f\"appear in {f.relative_to(site)}. Drafts must never leave the \"\n                    \"database. The site was not written.\")\n\n\ndef render(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> tuple[Path, list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Render Published content into `out`; return it and a list of rendered items.\n\n    Builds in a sibling folder and swaps it in only after the Draft check\n    passes, so a refusal never leaves a half-finished site.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    work = out.parent / (out.name + \".building\")\n    if work.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(work)\n    work.mkdir(parents=True)\n    try:\n        items = _render_into(work, database)\n    except BaseException:\n        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)\n        raise\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    work.rename(out)\n    return out, items\n\n\ndef _render_into(work: Path, database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    env = environment()\n    published = _published_posts(database)\n    entries = []\n    for post in published:\n        body = render_markdown(post[\"body\"])\n        entries.append({\n            \"id\": post[\"id\"], \"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": body,\n            \"excerpt\": _first_paragraph(body),\n            \"date\": (post[\"published_at\"] or \"\")[:10],\n            \"byline\": format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                                    post[\"byline_term\"]),\n            \"href\": f\"posts/{post['slug']}.html\",\n        })\n\n    (work / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (work / \"posts\").mkdir()\n    nav = {\"home_path\": \"index.html\", \"news_path\": \"news.html\"}\n\n    def write(path: str, template: str, prefix: str, **ctx) -> None:\n        # prefix is \"\" for top-level pages and \"../\" for pages in posts/\n        (work / path).write_text(env.get_template(template).render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, subtitle=settings.SITE_SUBTITLE,\n            css_path=prefix + \"style.css\",\n            home_path=prefix + nav[\"home_path\"], news_path=prefix + nav[\"news_path\"],\n            **ctx))\n\n    write(\"index.html\", \"public/home.html\", \"\", items=entries[:5])\n    write(\"news.html\", \"public/news.html\", \"\", items=entries)\n    for e in entries:\n        write(e[\"href\"], \"public/post.html\", \"../\", post=e)\n    _check_no_drafts(work, database)\n    return [\"Home (index.html)\", \"News (news.html)\"] + [\n        f\"Post: {e['title']} ({e['href']})\" for e in entries]\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    return render(out, database)[0]\n\"\"\"Markdown -> sanitized HTML. Preview and the Publish run both call this.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": True})\n\n_TAGS = {\"p\", \"br\", \"h1\", \"h2\", \"h3\", \"h4\", \"h5\", \"h6\", \"strong\", \"em\", \"ul\", \"ol\",\n         \"li\", \"blockquote\", \"a\", \"code\", \"pre\", \"hr\"}\n_ATTRIBUTES = {\"a\": {\"href\", \"title\"}}\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render Markdown, then strip everything outside the allowlist.\n\n    Raw HTML is removed (scripts, event handlers, images). Links keep only\n    http, https and mailto URLs and always get rel=\"noopener noreferrer\".\n    \"\"\"\n    return nh3.clean(\n        _md.render(text or \"\"),\n        tags=_TAGS,\n        attributes=_ATTRIBUTES,\n        url_schemes={\"http\", \"https\", \"mailto\"},\n        link_rel=\"noopener noreferrer\",\n    )\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a>{% if subtitle %} <span class=\"subtitle\">{{ subtitle }}</span>{% endif %}</header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"{{ url_for('list_view') }}\">All posts</a></p>\n  {% if post %}\n    <p>Status: <strong data-status=\"{{ post.status }}\">{{ post.status | capitalize }}</strong>\n      · Author: {{ post.author_name }}\n      · Created {{ post.created_at }} · Updated {{ post.updated_at }}\n      {% if post.published_at %}· Published {{ post.published_at }}{% endif %}</p>\n    {% if byline %}<p>Byline: {{ byline }}</p>{% endif %}\n  {% endif %}\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/posts/' ~ post.id if post else '/admin/posts' }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    {% if post %}<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"post_id\" value=\"{{ post.id }}\">{% endif %}\n    <p><label>Title <input name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form.title }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Slug <input name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ form.slug }}\"{% if not can_edit_slug %} readonly{% endif %}></label>\n      {% if not can_edit_slug %}<small>Locked once a Post has been Published. Ask the Admin to unlock it.</small>{% endif %}</p>\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br><textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"16\" cols=\"70\">{{ form.body }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <p>\n      <button type=\"submit\" name=\"action\" value=\"save\">Save</button>\n      <button type=\"submit\" formaction=\"/admin/posts/preview\" formtarget=\"_blank\">Preview</button>\n      {% if not post or post.status == 'draft' %}<button type=\"submit\" name=\"action\" value=\"publish\">Mark Published</button>{% endif %}\n      {% if post and post.status == 'published' %}<button type=\"submit\" name=\"action\" value=\"draft\">Set back to Draft</button>{% endif %}\n    </p>\n  </form>\n  {% if can_unlock_slug %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/unlock-slug\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Unlock Slug</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n  {% if can_delete %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Delete post</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Posts</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"{{ url_for('new_form') }}\">New post</a> · <a href=\"{{ url_for('admin_home') }}\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if not posts %}<p>No posts yet.</p>{% endif %}\n  <ul>\n  {% for p in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <strong data-status=\"{{ p.status }}\">{{ p.status | capitalize }}</strong>\n      {% if can_edit(user, p) %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('edit_form', post_id=p.id) }}\">{{ p.title }}</a>{% else %}{{ p.title }}{% endif %}\n      <small>by {{ p.author_name }}</small>\n    </li>\n  {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <p role=\"note\"><strong>Draft preview, not public</strong></p>\n  <article>\n    <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n    <p><em>{{ byline }}</em></p>\n    {{ html | safe }}\n  </article>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Users</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"{{ url_for('user_new') }}\">New user</a> · <a href=\"{{ url_for('admin_home') }}\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  <ul>\n  {% for u in users %}\n    <li>\n      <strong data-status=\"{{ 'active' if u.is_active else 'deactivated' }}\">{{ 'Active' if u.is_active else 'Deactivated' }}</strong>\n      <a href=\"{{ url_for('user_edit', user_id=u.id) }}\">{{ u.display_name }}</a>\n      — {{ u.role | capitalize }}{% if u.title %}, {{ u.title }}{% endif %}{% if u.term %}, {{ u.term }}{% endif %}\n      <small>{{ u.email }}</small>\n    </li>\n  {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n  <h2>Contact line for blocked Editors</h2>\n  <p><small>Shown to an Editor who opens an Admin-only page. It is never published to the site.</small></p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users/contact\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <p><label>Contact line <input name=\"contact_line\" value=\"{{ contact_line }}\" size=\"50\"></label>\n    <button type=\"submit\">Save</button></p>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Admin only</h1>\n  <p>You do not have permission to use this page.</p>\n  {% if admin %}<p>To ask for access, contact the current Admin, {{ admin.display_name }}{% if admin.title %} ({{ admin.title }}){% endif %}.</p>{% endif %}\n  {% if contact_line %}<p>{{ contact_line }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <p><a href=\"{{ url_for('admin_home') }}\">Back to the console</a></p>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}{% if subtitle %} <small>{{ subtitle }}</small>{% endif %}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    {% for item in items %}\n    <article data-post-id=\"{{ item.id }}\">\n      <h2><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></h2>\n      <p><small>{{ item.date }}</small></p>\n      {{ item.excerpt | safe }}\n    </article>\n    {% endfor %}\n    <p><a href=\"{{ news_path | default('news.html') }}\">All news</a></p>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>News</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li data-post-id=\"{{ item.id }}\"><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a> <small>{{ item.date }}</small></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block title %}{{ post.title {"parentUuid":"81d88117-6025-4d32-8d88-f5631d6bd9c3","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9100aa34-3880-4efe-933a-856c1a2fa5c8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KHGNAszMvNSomZwMhvAULg","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\nEvery app built here runs against its own temporary database, seeded with the\ndemo users below.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\",\n              \"display_name\": \"Demo Admin\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\",\n               \"display_name\": \"Demo Editor\"},\n}\n\n\ndef token_from(html: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token field\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    users.init_db(path)\n    for role, info in DEMO_USERS.items():\n        users.create_user(path, email=info[\"email\"], password=info[\"password\"],\n                          role=role, display_name=info[\"display_name\"])\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_client(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Build a fresh, anonymous client against the temporary database.\"\"\"\n\n    def _make() -> TestClient:\n        return TestClient(create_app(database=db_path))\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_client) -> TestClient:\n    return make_client()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_client):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = make_client()\n        form = c.get(\"/login\")\n        if form.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token_from(form.text)},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import posts, settings, users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    for email, name, role, password in (\n        (\"admin@example.test\", \"Demo Admin\", \"admin\", admin_pw),\n        (\"editor@example.test\", \"Demo Editor\", \"editor\", editor_pw),\n    ):\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=email,\n                              password=password, role=role, display_name=name)\n            print(f\"Created {role} {email}\")\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{email} already exists; left as is\")\n    posts.init_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    editor = users.authenticate(settings.DATABASE_PATH, \"editor@example.test\",\n                                editor_pw)\n    existing = {p[\"slug\"] for p in posts.list_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)}\n    for title, body, publish in (\n        (\"Welcome to the new chapter site\",\n         \"The **Chi Chapter** now keeps its own news. Read more on our \"\n         \"[chapter page](https://example.org).\", True),\n        (\"Rush announcement (draft)\", \"Dates still to be confirmed.\", False),\n    ):\n        if posts.slugify(title) in existing:\n            continue\n        posts.create_post(settings.DATABASE_PATH, author=editor, title=title,\n                          slug=\"\", body=body, publish=publish)\n        print(f\"Created {'Published' if publish else 'Draft'} post: {title}\")\n    # TODO (later tickets): demo Pages.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"T02: Posts, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\n\ndef save_post(c, title=\"Rush night\", body=\"Hello **world**\", slug=\"\", action=\"save\",\n              post_id=None, page=\"/admin/posts/new\", **extra):\n    \"\"\"Submit the post form; return the response (redirects not followed).\"\"\"\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    token = token_from(c.get(page).text)\n    return c.post(path, data={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body,\n                              \"action\": action, \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef created_id(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_form(c, post_id, path_suffix, **data):\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}{path_suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": token, **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\",\n                           \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- create, slug, list ---------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_with_generated_slug(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"spring-rush-kickoff\" in page\n    assert \"Draft\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page  # author\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_duplicate_slug_is_refused_with_a_clear_error(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Same Title\"))\n\"\"\"T03: `cms publish`, tested at render_site() and the CLI.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import posts, users\nfrom app.cli import main\nfrom app.publish import DraftInOutput, render_site\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef author(db_path):\n    posts.init_posts(db_path)\n    return users.get_user(db_path, 2)  # the seeded editor\n\n\ndef add(db_path, author, title, body=\"Body text.\", slug=\"\", publish=True):\n    return posts.create_post(db_path, author=author, title=title, slug=slug,\n                             body=body, publish=publish)\n\n\ndef site_text(out):\n    return {p: p.read_text() for p in out.rglob(\"*\") if p.is_file()}\n\n\ndef test_home_news_and_post_pages(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, author, \"Rush Night\", body=\"First para.\\n\\nSecond para.\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    home = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in home and \"First para.\" in home\n    assert \"Second para.\" not in home\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in (out / \"news.html\").read_text()\n    page = (out / \"posts\" / \"rush-night.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page and \"Second para.\" in page\n\n\ndef test_home_shows_only_5_latest_news_lists_all_newest_first(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    for i in range(7):\n        pid = add(db_path, author, f\"Story {i}\")\n        with posts.connect(db_path) as conn:\n            conn.execute(\"update posts set published_at=? where id=?\",\n                         (f\"2026-01-0{i + 1}T00:00:00+00:00\", pid))\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    home = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Story 6\" in home and \"Story 2\" in home\n    assert \"Story 1\" not in home and \"Story 0\" not in home\n    news = (out / \"news.html\").read_text()\n    assert news.index(\"Story 6\") < news.index(\"Story 3\") < news.index(\"Story 0\")\n\n\ndef test_drafts_are_absent(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, author, \"Secret Draft Title\", slug=\"secret-slug\", publish=False)\n    add(db_path, author, \"Public One\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    for text in site_text(out).values():\n        assert \"Secret Draft Title\" not in text and \"secret-slug\" not in text\n    assert not (out / \"posts\" / \"secret-slug.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_post_set_back_to_draft_is_absent_next_render(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `GLOSSARY.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"19d51020-d967-469f-b060-73ead07a6b2e","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:14:47.648Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\nEvery app built here runs against its own temporary database, seeded with the\ndemo users below.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\",\n              \"display_name\": \"Demo Admin\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\",\n               \"display_name\": \"Demo Editor\"},\n}\n\n\ndef token_from(html: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token field\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    users.init_db(path)\n    for role, info in DEMO_USERS.items():\n        users.create_user(path, email=info[\"email\"], password=info[\"password\"],\n                          role=role, display_name=info[\"display_name\"])\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_client(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Build a fresh, anonymous client against the temporary database.\"\"\"\n\n    def _make() -> TestClient:\n        return TestClient(create_app(database=db_path))\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_client) -> TestClient:\n    return make_client()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_client):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = make_client()\n        form = c.get(\"/login\")\n        if form.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token_from(form.text)},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import posts, settings, users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    for email, name, role, password in (\n        (\"admin@example.test\", \"Demo Admin\", \"admin\", admin_pw),\n        (\"editor@example.test\", \"Demo Editor\", \"editor\", editor_pw),\n    ):\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=email,\n                              password=password, role=role, display_name=name)\n            print(f\"Created {role} {email}\")\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{email} already exists; left as is\")\n    posts.init_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    editor = users.authenticate(settings.DATABASE_PATH, \"editor@example.test\",\n                                editor_pw)\n    existing = {p[\"slug\"] for p in posts.list_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)}\n    for title, body, publish in (\n        (\"Welcome to the new chapter site\",\n         \"The **Chi Chapter** now keeps its own news. Read more on our \"\n         \"[chapter page](https://example.org).\", True),\n        (\"Rush announcement (draft)\", \"Dates still to be confirmed.\", False),\n    ):\n        if posts.slugify(title) in existing:\n            continue\n        posts.create_post(settings.DATABASE_PATH, author=editor, title=title,\n                          slug=\"\", body=body, publish=publish)\n        print(f\"Created {'Published' if publish else 'Draft'} post: {title}\")\n    # TODO (later tickets): demo Pages.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"T02: Posts, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main page=\"/admin/posts/new\", **extra):\n    \"\"\"Submit the post form; return the response (redirects not followed).\"\"\"\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    token = token_from(c.get(page).text)\n    return c.post(path, data={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body,\n                              \"action\": action, \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef created_id(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_form(c, post_id, path_suffix, **data):\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    return token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\",\n                           \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- create, slug, list ---------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_with_generated_slug(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = {"parentUuid":"7c190dd5-4470-408e-9673-f9033dc04ad8","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"hook_success","hookName":"PreToolUse:Bash","toolUseID":"toolu_019qARDqao6iVQW4z7nZWcNA","hookEvent":"PreToolUse","content":"","stdout":"{\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"hookEventName\":\"PreToolUse\",\"permissionDecisionReason\":\"RTK auto-rewrite\",\"updatedInput\":{\"command\":\"rtk read app/cli.py templates/admin/hello.html app/settings.py; sed -n 60,140p tests/test_publish.py; rtk grep -n \\\"deactivate\\\" tests/*.py | head; uv run rtk pytest -q 2>&1 | tail -3\"}}}\n","stderr":"","exitCode":0,"command":"rtk hook claude","durationMs":63},"type":"attachment","uuid":"3b0b84c7-a7ae-4051-b90a-b7c75d0a43fe","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:15:13.430Z","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7000aa97-0f97-4842-acdc-fc51b49f56dc","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"7c190dd5-4470-408e-9673-f9033dc04ad8","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"9100aa34-3880-4efe-933a-856c1a2fa5c8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019qARDqao6iVQW4z7nZWcNA","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n    uv run cms create-admin EMAIL   # make an Admin; asks for the password\n    uv run cms reset-password EMAIL # reset any User's password (hidden prompt)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport getpass\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import deploy, settings, users\nfrom app.publish import DraftInOutput, render\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, push=deploy.push_site,\n         confirm=input) -> int:\n    \"\"\"`push` and `confirm` are seams: tests stub them so nothing touches gh-pages.\"\"\"\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy_cmd = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy_cmd.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    create = sub.add_parser(\"create-admin\", help=\"create an Admin account\")\n    create.add_argument(\"email\")\n    create.add_argument(\"--name\", default=\"Admin\", help=\"display name\")\n    reset = sub.add_parser(\"reset-password\",\n                           help=\"reset a User's password (works for the Admin)\")\n    reset.add_argument(\"email\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"reset-password\":\n        users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n        target = users.get_user_by_email(settings.DATABASE_PATH, args.email)\n        if target is None:\n            print(f\"No user with the email {args.email}.\")\n            return 1\n        print(f\"Passwords must be at least {users.MIN_PASSWORD} characters.\")\n        password = getpass.getpass(\"New password: \")\n        if password != getpass.getpass(\"New password again: \"):\n            print(\"The passwords did not match; nothing was changed.\")\n            return 1\n        problem = users.password_problem(password)\n        if problem:\n            print(f\"{problem} Nothing was changed.\")\n            return 1\n        users.set_password(settings.DATABASE_PATH, target[\"id\"], password)\n        print(f\"Reset the password for {target['email']}.\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"create-admin\":\n        print(f\"Passwords must be at least {users.MIN_PASSWORD} characters.\")\n        password = getpass.getpass(\"Password: \")\n        if password != getpass.getpass(\"Password again: \"):\n            print(\"The passwords did not match; nothing was created.\")\n            return 1\n        users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=args.email,\n                              password=password, role=\"admin\",\n                              display_name=args.name)\n        except ValueError as exc:\n            print(f\"{exc} Nothing was created.\")\n            return 1\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{args.email} already has an account.\")\n            return 1\n        print(f\"Created Admin {args.email}.\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        if settings.SECRET_KEY == settings.DEV_SECRET_KEY:\n            print(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY is not set (copy .env.example to .env and \"\n                  \"change it). Refusing to serve with the public dev key.\")\n            return 1\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        try:\n            out, items = render()\n        except DraftInOutput as exc:\n            print(exc)\n            return 1\n        print(\"Rendered:\")\n        for item in items:\n            print(f\"  - {item}\")\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        bad = deploy.root_absolute_files(settings.SITE)\n        if bad:\n            print(\"Refusing to deploy: root-absolute paths (href=\\\"/...\\\") in \"\n                  + \", \".join(str(b) for b in bad)\n                  + \". Run `uv run cms publish` again.\")\n            return 1\n        if confirm(\"Push site/ to gh-pages and make it Live? [y/N] \").strip().lower() not in (\"y\", \"yes\"):\n            print(\"Not deployed. Nothing was pushed.\")\n            return 0\n        code = push(settings.SITE, args.message)\n        if code != 0:\n            print(\"The push failed, so nothing was recorded as deployed.\")\n            return code\n        deploy.record_deploy(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n        print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. In GitHub: Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a \"\n              \"branch -> gh-pages / root. It can take up to 10 minutes to appear.\")\n        return 0\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>T00: hello admin</h1>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.display_name }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n  <p><a href=\"{{ url_for('list_view') }}\">Posts</a>{% if user.role == 'admin' %} · <a href=\"{{   uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n    uv run cms create-admin EMAIL   # make an Admin; asks for the password\n    uv run cms reset-password EMAIL # reset any User's password (hidden
